**First customer message:** @VirginTrains hi there. I bought tickets for travel from Piccadilly to Euston on 15/12/17. Advance singles. Online from the Trainline. What happens if strike goes ahead. Do I get a refund?

1. **CUSTOMER** `527357` 2017-12-02 07:28: @VirginTrains hi there. I bought tickets for travel from Piccadilly to Euston on 15/12/17. Advance singles. Online from the Trainline. What happens if strike goes ahead. Do I get a refund?
